  ⎿  Plan saved to: ~/.claude/plans/curried-prancing-russell.md · /plan to edit
     Plan: Truth Layer, Recovery Fixes, and Analytics (post-review hardening)

     Context

     A review of the swarm (2026-07-06) found the system optimizes for "tasks
     completed" rather than "games that work." Concrete evidence from this week:
     two total-showstopper bugs (dead signal connections, HUD swallowing all
     clicks) survived every verification layer and were found by a human; a
     playthrough_bot task was marked completed while the agent's own log claimed
     failure; the runtime's truncation-retry mechanism burned ~15 loops without
     recovering; the API server ran 2 days on stale code with no warning.

     Code exploration confirmed and worsened the diagnosis:

     - Free success at loop limit: agent_runtime.py:1388-1392 sets
     task_complete_hit = TASK_TYPE != "playthrough_bot" or ... — every
     non-playthrough task type that hits MAX_TOOL_LOOPS exits as SUCCESS.
     - Bare-marker success: agent_finish.py:79-92 _classify_agent_success
     treats exit 0 OR a TASK_COMPLETE log marker as success. No evidence
     (commit/diff/test) is required for any type except playthrough_bot
     (contractor's guard, commit 6f405979).
     - One-shot validation block: the "TASK_COMPLETE blocked" mechanism
     (agent_runtime.py:1361-1375) clears _last_run_outputs after rejecting
     once (:1373), so a second bare TASK_COMPLETE passes.
     - Unattributed diff evidence: _capture_project_diff_stat
     (agent_finish.py:182-196) runs git diff --stat HEAD~1 in the main
     project dir — shows the latest commit regardless of author; a no-op agent
     inherits the previous commit's diff as false evidence.
     - Truncation retry unbounded in practice: cap counts only consecutive
     truncations and resets on any parseable response (agent_runtime.py:1154- 
     1156, reset at :1227). Alternating truncated/valid responses never trip
     it — the exact 15-loop burn observed. The invalid-JSON retry branch
     (:1168-1176) has no cap at all. Cosmetic: log says ({n}/2) while
     code allows 3 (:1159).
     - Analytics blind spots: wrap-up warning logs nothing; meta-investigation
     ([Meta]), vision cap ([VisionCap]), operator hint ([Hint]), and plan
     budget are unmatched by extract_signals() in swarm/log_rotation.py.
     - No stale-code detection: /api/health (swarm/api_agents.py:198-214)
     reports nothing about which commit is running.

     User decisions (2026-07-07): enforcement = hard for loop-limit, soft-flag
     for no-commit (flip to hard after ~a week of data); analytics = full
     (API + dashboard panel).

     Precondition: contractor collision management

     The contractor has UNCOMMITTED changes in swarm/agent_runtime.py,
     prompts/playthrough_bot.yaml, swarm/tools/playthrough_kit.py,
     tests/test_agent_runtime.py (their lane: playthrough completion, handoff
     items 1-3). Phases B and A below also touch agent_runtime.py.

     Rule: do Phases D, C, E first (no overlap). Before starting A/B, check
     git status — if contractor changes are still uncommitted in
     agent_runtime.py, coordinate with the user before proceeding. Never touch
     prompts/playthrough_bot.yaml or playthrough_kit.py (contractor lane).

     Phase D — Version stamp in /api/health (do first, ~30 min)

     - In create_app() (swarm/api.py): capture git rev-parse --short HEAD
     once at startup → module-level _running_commit.
     - In /api/health (swarm/api_agents.py:198): add running_commit, plus
     repo_commit from a fresh git rev-parse cached with 60s TTL, and
     code_stale: bool (running != repo).
     - Dashboard: small warning chip in the header when code_stale (follow the
     existing health-polling pattern in dashboard.js).

     Phase B — Recovery machinery: fix + instrument

     Fixes (swarm/agent_runtime.py)

     1. Total truncation budget: keep the consecutive cap (3) but add a
     per-run total budget _TRUNCATION_TOTAL_BUDGET = 8; exceeding either
     fails the task with a distinct failure reason ("llm truncation budget
     exhausted"). The consecutive counter still resets at :1227; the total
     counter never resets.
     2. Cap the invalid-JSON retry branch (:1168-1176): share the same total
     budget counter (they're the same underlying failure class).
     3. Fix the ({n}/2) vs 3 log mismatch (:1159).
     4. Sticky validation block: in the TASK_COMPLETE-blocked path (:1373 and
     the :1188-1204 twin), do NOT clear _last_run_outputs on rejection.
     Instead clear it only when a subsequent run_command output contains no
     _FAILURE_PATTERNS match (i.e. evidence the failure was fixed).

     Instrumentation (make every mechanism visible to analytics)

     5. Add distinctive log lines where missing: wrap-up ([WrapUp] injected at 
     loop N), plan budget ([PlanBudget] warning at loop N). Meta/VisionCap/
     Hint/stall already log; standardize nothing else.
     6. swarm/log_rotation.py extract_signals(): add regexes for [Meta],
     [VisionCap], [WrapUp], [PlanBudget], [Hint], stall redirect, and
     truncation/invalid-JSON retries (distinct from generic WARNING capture).
     Accumulate into ONE new column: mechanism_fires TEXT (JSON dict, e.g.
     {"truncation_retry": 4, "stall": 1, "wrap_up": 1}).
     7. swarm/db.py: add mechanism_fires column via the existing
     _evolve_schema() PRAGMA-guard pattern; include in
     agent_signals_upsert() column list.

     New tests in tests/test_log_rotation.py (mechanism extraction) and
     tests/test_agent_runtime.py (budget caps — coordinate with contractor's
     uncommitted tests in that file).

     Phase A — Completion truth layer

     A1. Canonical task-type map (swarm/constants.py)

     WRITE_TASK_TYPES = {"feature", "bug", "refactor", "polish", "art_pass",
                         "integration", "playthrough_bot"}
     # everything else (qa, research, plan, audit, project_plan, ...) is
     # report/orchestration output — no commit evidence expected

     A2. Evidence capture (swarm/agent_lifecycle.py + swarm/agent_finish.py)

     - At spawn: record the project's current HEAD (git rev-parse HEAD) into
     agent metadata (head_at_spawn). Spawn already writes agent rows —
     piggyback there.
     - At finish (in _finish_agent, near the existing diff capture :798):
     build completion_evidence dict:
     {new_commits: int (rev-list head_at_spawn..HEAD), commit_hash,   diff_stat 
     (now attributed: diff head_at_spawn..HEAD),   validation_passed, exit_code,
     log_marker_present}.
     Store it in BOTH the task's metadata.completion_evidence and the agent
     row metadata. Replace the misleading HEAD~1 diff with the attributed one
     when head_at_spawn exists (keep HEAD~1 as fallback for legacy rows).

     A3. Enforcement

     - Hard (now) — close the loop-limit hole: at agent_runtime.py:1388-1392,
     loop-limit exit sets task_complete_hit = False for ALL types (delete the
     playthrough-only carve-out; a task that burns 200 loops without declaring
     completion is a failure, full stop). This makes loop-limit exits go
     through normal retry/escalation instead of fake success.
     - Soft (now, flip later) — no-commit flag: in _finish_agent, when a
     WRITE_TASK_TYPES task is about to be marked completed with
     new_commits == 0, log [Evidence] WARNING: write-type task completed with 
     zero commits since spawn, set
     metadata.completion_evidence.unverified = true, and increment nothing
     else. After ~a week, flipping to hard = changing one branch to route into
     the existing failure path.
     - Surface: analytics endpoint (Phase C) exposes the unverified-completion
     count per project so the flip decision is data-driven.

     Phase C — Analytics layer (#7): API + dashboard panel

     New module swarm/analytics.py (queries) + swarm/api_analytics.py (routes)

     Register in swarm/api.py like the other api_* modules. Endpoints:

     1. GET /api/analytics/overview — global + per-project: completed/failed
     counts, total & avg estimated_cost_usd per completed task (agents table,
     populated at agent_finish.py:811), token totals, avg loops.
     2. GET /api/analytics/value-repair?project= — the run-11 hand analysis as
     a live query: value tasks (feature/polish/art_pass completed) vs repair
     tasks (bug where metadata.is_validation_bug or attempts > 1) → ratio
     per project. Verify against run-11's known 2.8x for the art arm.
     3. GET /api/analytics/deaths — from agent_signals: terminal_status
     breakdown, avg loop_count at death by task_type, top error_snippets.
     4. GET /api/analytics/mechanisms — join mechanism_fires (Phase B) with
     terminal_status: per mechanism, fire count and completion rate with vs
     without fires. This is the "do our recovery reflexes actually help" view.
     5. GET /api/analytics/ship-candidates — per Godot project: closure_status,
     playthrough smoke-check presence/last result (closure tables),
     validation-bug rate (last 50 tasks), unverified-completion count (Phase
     A3) → ranked list of "closest to shippable." This is the enabler for
     actually releasing one game.

     Dashboard panel

     - New collapsible "Analytics" section in dashboard.html following the
     existing metrics-panel / metrics-grid pattern (plain cells + HTML
     tables, no chart library).
     - Fetch/render in dashboard-config.js mirroring loadMetrics()
     (dashboard-config.js:925): poll /api/analytics/overview +
     /api/analytics/value-repair on the same 30s cadence; deaths,
     mechanisms, ship-candidates load on-demand when the section expands.

     Tests: tests/test_analytics.py — seed tasks/agents/agent_signals rows in an
     isolated DB (reuse tests/test_log_rotation.py's fixture pattern), assert
     each endpoint's math, including a value/repair fixture reproducing a known
     ratio.

     Phase E — Victory-condition requirement (prompts only)

     - prompts/project_plan.yaml and prompts/project_create.yaml: require
     GAME_DESIGN.md to contain a ## Victory Condition section stating a
     machine-checkable end state, and require get_game_state() to expose the
     field(s) needed to detect it (mirrors the existing "MUST contain a
     '## Controls' section" rule at project_plan.yaml:40).
     - Do NOT touch prompts/playthrough_bot.yaml (contractor lane) — their
     prompt already consumes GAME_DESIGN.md win conditions; this phase makes
     those conditions exist and be checkable for future projects.

     Explicitly out of scope

     - Playthrough bot completion itself (contractor: handoff items 1-3; run-12
     stays paused until their clean run).
     - Auto-seeding playthrough closure gates across all projects (deferred
     until
     contractor's clean run proves the pattern).
     - Event-driven scheduling core (roadmap #9) — deliberately deferred.
     - Actually shipping a game — operational follow-up once
     /api/analytics/ship-candidates exists to pick the candidate.

     Suggested order

     D (30 min) → C (no agent_runtime overlap, biggest value) → E (prompts) →
     B → A (both touch agent_runtime.py; sync with contractor first).

     Verification

     1. pytest -q green after every phase (baseline 1460 + new tests).
     2. Phase D: curl /api/health shows running_commit; make a dummy commit,
     confirm code_stale: true within 60s.
     3. Phase B: synthetic log fixtures — alternating truncated/valid responses
     trip the total budget; extract_signals returns correct
     mechanism_fires counts.
     4. Phase A: spawn a real low-risk task (e.g. a docs tweak on
     swarm-test-project); confirm metadata.completion_evidence.new_commits >= 1.
     Simulate a no-op completion in a unit test; confirm unverified: true
     flag and the [Evidence] log line. Unit-test that loop-limit exit now
     produces failure for a feature-type task.
     5. Phase C: value-repair endpoint for the run-11 art project reproduces
     ~2.8x (±rounding) against the hand analysis in
     docs/experiment-designs/run11-analysis.md; dashboard panel renders with
     live data.
     6. Phase E: run the project_plan prompt render test (like the existing
     playthrough render check) — no undefined variables; text includes the
     Victory Condition requirement.

⏺ Starting Phase D. First, the version capture in create_app():
